\mathbf{C}^{j,j^{\prime},j}_{1,1}=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle^{2}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big]
.

2.

\mathbf{C}^{j,j^{\prime},j}_{i_{1},i_{1}}=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{1}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle^{2}\big]=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{1}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle^{2}\big]=\sigma_{x}^{2}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big]
, for all
i_{1}\neq 1
.

3.

\mathbf{C}^{j,j^{\prime},j}_{1,i_{2}}=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{2}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{2}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]=0
, for all
i_{2}\neq 1
.

4.

\mathbf{C}^{j,j^{\prime},j}_{i_{1},1}=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{1}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{1}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]=0
, for all
i_{1}\neq 1
.

5.

\mathbf{C}^{j,j^{\prime},j}_{i_{1},i_{2}}=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{1}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{2}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{1}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{2}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]=0
, for all
i_{1},i_{2}\neq 1
and
i_{1}\neq i_{2}
.

Case II:
j\neq j^{\prime}
.

1.

\mathbf{C}^{j,j^{\prime},j^{\prime\prime}}_{1,1}=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]
.

2.

\mathbf{C}^{j,j^{\prime},j^{\prime\prime}}_{1,i_{2}}=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{2}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{2}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]=0
, for all
i_{2}\neq 1
.

3.

\mathbf{C}^{j,j^{\prime},j^{\prime\prime}}_{i_{1},1}=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{1}},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{1}},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle\big]=0
, for all
i_{1}\neq 1
.

4.

\mathbf{C}^{j,j^{\prime},j^{\prime\prime}}_{i_{1},i_{2}}=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{1}},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{2}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]=\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{1}},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle\big]\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\bm{\xi}_{i_{2}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]=0
, for all
i_{1},i_{2}\neq 1
.

By previous discussion of
\mathbf{C}^{j,j^{\prime},j^{\prime\prime}}_{i_{1},i_{2}}
, we derive that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\alpha^{(1)}}{D^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\neq j^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]\mathbf{v}^{*}\mathbf{v}^{*\top}+\frac{\alpha^{(1)}(D-1)\sigma_{x}^{2}}{D^{2}}\sum_{i=2}^{d}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]\bm{\xi}_{i}\bm{\xi}_{i}^{\top}.

(B.11)

Notice that the coefficients of
\bm{\xi}_{i}\bm{\xi}_{i}^{\top}
are all equal in both
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
. Besides, we define two sets:
J_{1}=\{(j,j^{\prime})|j,j^{\prime}\in[D];j,j^{\prime}\neq j^{*},j\neq j^{\prime}\}
and
J_{2}=\{(j,j^{\prime},j^{\prime\prime})|j,j^{\prime},j^{\prime\prime}\in[D];j,j^{\prime},j^{\prime\prime}\neq j^{*},j\neq j^{\prime}\neq j^{\prime\prime}\}
. Then by using (
B.10
) minus (
B.11
), we get

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{W}_{1,1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(1)},\mathbf{W}^{(1)})=I_{1}-I_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{(1)}}{D^{2}}\Bigg(\underbrace{(D-1)\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]-\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\neq j^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]}_{I_{3}}\Bigg)\mathbf{v}^{*}\mathbf{v}^{*\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{(1)}}{D^{2}}\Bigg(\underbrace{(D-1)\sum_{(j,j^{\prime})\in J_{1}}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]}_{I_{3,1}}-\underbrace{\sum_{(j,j^{\prime},j^{\prime\prime})\in J_{2}}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]}_{I_{3,2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{(D-1)\sum_{(j,j^{\prime})\notin J_{1}}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]-\sum_{(j,j^{\prime},j^{\prime\prime})\notin J_{2}}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]}_{I_{3,3}}\Bigg)\mathbf{v}^{*}\mathbf{v}^{*\top}.

By carefully checking the terms inner expectation of
I_{3,1}
, we can utilize Lemma
D.12
to obtain that

\displaystyle-\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{4(\eta^{2}\sigma_{x}^{2}+1)\eta\sigma_{x}^{5}}{\pi}e^{\frac{3\eta^{2}\sigma_{x}^{4}}{4\pi}}D^{5/2}\leq I_{3,1}\leq-\frac{1}{48\pi\eta^{4}\sigma_{x}^{5}}e^{-\frac{\eta\sigma_{x}^{2}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}-\frac{2\pi}{\eta^{2}\sigma_{x}^{4}}-\frac{1}{2}}D^{5/2}.

Similarly, we obtain that

\displaystyle-\frac{16}{\eta\sigma_{x}^{2}}e^{\frac{\eta^{2}\sigma_{x}^{4}}{4\pi}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma_{x}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}+\frac{\eta\sigma_{x}^{3}e^{\frac{\eta^{2}\sigma_{x}^{4}}{4\pi}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\bigg)^{3}D^{5/2}\leq I_{3,2}\leq\frac{16}{\eta\sigma_{x}^{2}}e^{\frac{\eta^{2}\sigma_{x}^{4}}{4\pi}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma_{x}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}+\frac{\eta\sigma_{x}^{3}e^{\frac{\eta^{2}\sigma_{x}^{4}}{4\pi}}}{\sqrt{2\pi}}\bigg)^{3}D^{5/2}

by Lemma
D.13
,
and

\displaystyle|I_{3,3}|\leq 6\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\sigma_{x}^{3}D^{2}

by Lemma
D.14
. Applying all the preceding results to the gradient descent iteration of
\mathbf{W}_{1,1}^{(t)}
, we finally obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}_{1,1}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{W}_{1,1}^{(1)}-\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{W}_{1,1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(1)},\mathbf{W}^{(1)})=\beta_{1}\mathbf{v}^{*}\mathbf{v}^{*\top}.

And the coefficient
\beta_{1}
satisfy that
|\beta_{1}\|\leq c_{1}\sqrt{D}
for some non-negative constant
c_{1}
solely depending on
\eta
and
\sigma_{x}
. This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma B.12
.

Under the same condition with Theorem
3.2
, the iterates
\mathbf{W}_{2,2}^{(t)}
of gradient descent defined in (
3.4
) satisfies that

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}_{2,2}^{(2)}=\beta_{2}\Big(\sum_{j\neq j^{*}}\big(\mathbf{p}_{j^{*}}-\mathbf{p}_{j}\big)\Big)\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\bigg).

The coefficient
\beta_{2}
satisfy that
\frac{c_{2}}{D^{2}}\leq\beta_{2}\leq\frac{c_{3}}{D^{2}}
for some non-negative constants
c_{2},c_{3}
solely depending on
\eta
and
\sigma_{x}
.

Proof of Lemma
B.12
.

∎

For
\nabla_{\mathbf{W}_{2,2}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(1)},\mathbf{W}^{(1)})
, we can derive that

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{W}_{2,2}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(1)},\mathbf{W}^{(1)})=\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(1)}\cdot y\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\not=j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(1)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(1)}\langle\mathbf{v}^{(1)},\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle(\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}-\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime\prime}})\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\frac{\alpha^{(1)}(D-1)}{D^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}\cdot y\cdot\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big]\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}}_{I_{1}}-\underbrace{\frac{\alpha^{(1)}}{D^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\not=j^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}\cdot y\cdot\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big]\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}}_{I_{2}}.

We discuss the value of
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
respectively in the following. For
I_{1}
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle I_{1}=\underbrace{\frac{\alpha^{(1)}(D-1)}{D^{2}}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big]\mathbf{p}_{j^{*}}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\bigg)}_{I_{1,1}}+\underbrace{\frac{\alpha^{(1)}(D-1)}{D^{2}}\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j^{*}}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big]\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\bigg)}_{I_{1,2}}.

While for
I_{2}
, we can also obtain that

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{\alpha^{(1)}}{D^{2}}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big]\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\neq j^{*}}\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\bigg)}_{I_{2,1}}+\underbrace{\frac{\alpha^{(1)}}{D^{2}}\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j^{*}}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big]\mathbf{p}_{j^{*}}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\bigg)}_{I_{2,2}}

\displaystyle\ +\underbrace{\frac{\alpha^{(1)}}{D^{2}}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\neq j^{*}}\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j^{\prime\prime},j^{*}}\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big]\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\bigg)}_{I_{2,3}}.

As we discussed earlier,
\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]
takes the same value for all
j\neq j^{*}
. Therefore, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{W}_{2,2}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(1)},\mathbf{W}^{(1)})=I_{1}-I_{2}=\big(I_{1,1}-I_{2,1}\big)+\Big(\big(I_{1,2}-I_{2,3}\big)-I_{2,2}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{(1)}}{D^{2}}\Big(\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big]-\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big]\Big)\Big(\sum_{j\neq j^{*}}\big(\mathbf{p}_{j^{*}}-\mathbf{p}_{j}\big)\Big)\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\bigg).

Furthermore, we can utilize Lemma
D.10
and Lemma
D.11
to obtain that,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big]-\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]\geq-\sigma_{x}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}-\frac{4(1+\eta\sigma_{x}^{2})}{\eta\sigma_{x}\sqrt{D\pi}}\exp\bigg(\frac{\eta^{2}\sigma_{x}^{4}}{2\pi}\bigg),

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big]-\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]\leq-\frac{\sigma_{x}}{2\sqrt{2e\pi}}e^{-\frac{\eta\sigma_{x}^{2}}{2\pi}}.

Applying all the preceding results to the gradient descent iteration of
\mathbf{W}_{2,2}^{(t)}
, we finally obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbf{W}_{2,2}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{W}_{2,2}^{(1)}-\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{W}_{2,2}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(1)},\mathbf{W}^{(1)})

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta\alpha^{(1)}}{D^{2}}\Big(-\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big]+\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime(1)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big]\Big)\Big(\sum_{j\neq j^{*}}\big(\mathbf{p}_{j^{*}}-\mathbf{p}_{j}\big)\Big)\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\beta_{2}\Big(\sum_{j\neq j^{*}}\big(\mathbf{p}_{j^{*}}-\mathbf{p}_{j}\big)\Big)\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\bigg),

where

\displaystyle\frac{1}{D^{2}}\frac{\eta^{2}\sigma_{x}^{2}}{4\pi\sqrt{e}}e^{-\frac{\eta\sigma_{x}^{2}}{2\pi}}\leq\beta_{2}\leq\frac{1}{D^{2}}\frac{\eta^{2}\sigma_{x}}{\pi}\bigg(\sigma_{x}+\frac{4(1+\eta\sigma_{x}^{2})}{\eta\sigma_{x}\sqrt{2D}}e^{\frac{\eta^{2}\sigma_{x}^{4}}{2\pi}}\bigg)\leq\frac{1}{D^{2}}\frac{2\eta^{2}\sigma_{x}^{2}}{\pi}.

This completes the proof.

B.4
Proof of Lemma
B.5
, Lemma
B.2
, Lemma
B.3
and Lemma
B.4

In this subsection, we provide complete proof for Lemma
B.5
, Lemma
B.2
, Lemma
B.3
and Lemma
B.4

We first prove Lemma
B.5
, given that the result concerning
\mathbf{W}^{(t)}
in Proposition
B.1
, Lemma
B.3
and Lemma
B.4
holds. Then
we use Lemma
B.5
to prove Lemma
B.2
, Lemma
B.3
and Lemma
B.4
by induction. We would like to clarify that this is not circular reasoning, since we are utilizing induction. It’s reasonable to assume that all conclusions hold for each iteration and verify all conclusions still hold for the next iteration, as long as we can rigorously demonstrate these conclusions hold at the beginning.

Proof of Lemma
B.5
.

By Lemma
B.3
and Lemma
B.4
, there exists constants
c_{1},c_{2}
solely depending on
\eta,\sigma_{x}
such that
|\beta_{1}|\leq c_{1}\sqrt{D}
and
\beta_{2}\geq c_{2}\frac{1}{D^{2}}
. Then further combining with Lemma
D.15
, Lemma
D.16
and Lemma
D.18
, with probability at least
1-\sqrt{\frac{26c_{1}\sigma_{x}^{2}}{c_{2}\pi}}De^{-\frac{c_{2}}{26c_{1}\sigma_{x}^{2}}\sqrt{D}}-De^{-\frac{D}{2}}
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{(t)}\mathbf{z}_{j}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{1,1}^{(t)}\mathbf{x}_{j}+\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{2,2}^{(t)}\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\beta_{1}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle-\frac{(D+1)^{2}}{4}\beta_{2}+\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{1,1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\mathbf{x}_{j}+\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{2,2,\text{error}}^{(t)}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}

\displaystyle\leq\big|\beta_{1}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big|+\big\|\mathbf{W}_{1,1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\|\mathbf{x}_{j}\|_{2}\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\|_{2}+\big\|\mathbf{W}_{2,2,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\|\mathbf{p}_{j}\|_{2}\|\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq c_{1}\sqrt{D}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{c_{2}}{13c_{1}\sigma_{x}^{2}}}\sigma_{x}D^{\frac{1}{4}}\Bigg)^{2}+\frac{1}{e^{C_{2}\sqrt{D}}}\sigma_{x}^{2}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})^{2}+\frac{1}{e^{C_{2}\sqrt{D}}}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{D+1}{2}}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{2}}{12}D

holds for all
j^{\prime}\neq j^{*}
and
j\in[D]
, and

\displaystyle\mathbf{z}_{j^{*}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{(T)}\mathbf{z}_{j}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{1,1}^{(T)}\mathbf{x}_{j}+\mathbf{p}_{j^{*}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{2,2}^{(T)}\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\beta_{1}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle+\frac{(D-1)(D+1)^{2}}{4}\beta_{2}+\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{1,1,\text{error}}^{(T)}\mathbf{x}_{j}+\mathbf{p}_{j^{*}}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{2,2,\text{error}}^{(T)}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{c_{2}D}{4}-\big|\beta_{1}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big|-\big\|\mathbf{W}_{1,1,\text{error}}^{(T)}\big\|_{2}\|\mathbf{x}_{j}\|_{2}\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\|_{2}-\big\|\mathbf{W}_{2,2,\text{error}}^{(T)}\big\|_{2}\|\mathbf{p}_{j}\|_{2}\|\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{c_{2}D}{4}-c_{1}\sqrt{D}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{c_{2}}{13c_{1}\sigma_{x}^{2}}}\sigma_{x}D^{\frac{1}{4}}\Bigg)^{2}-\frac{1}{e^{C_{2}\sqrt{D}}}\sigma_{x}^{2}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})^{2}-\frac{1}{e^{C_{2}\sqrt{D}}}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{D+1}{2}}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{c_{2}}{6}D

holds for all
j\in[D]
. Therefore, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(T)}\geq\frac{e^{\frac{c_{2}}{6}D}}{e^{\frac{c_{2}}{6}D}+(D-1)e^{\frac{c_{2}}{12}D}}\geq 1-De^{-\frac{c_{2}}{12}D};

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(T)}\leq\frac{e^{\frac{c_{2}}{12}D}}{e^{\frac{c_{2}}{6}D}+(D-1)e^{\frac{c_{2}}{12}D}}\leq e^{-\frac{c_{2}}{12}D},

holds for all
j^{\prime}\neq j^{*}
and
j\in[D]
with probability at least
1-\sqrt{\frac{26c_{1}\sigma_{x}^{2}}{c_{2}\pi}}D^{3/4}e^{-\frac{c_{2}}{26c_{1}\sigma_{x}^{2}}\sqrt{D}}-De^{-\frac{D}{2}}\geq 1-e^{-C_{8}\sqrt{D}}
for some non-negative constant
C_{8}
solely depending on
\eta,\sigma_{x}
.
∎

Next, we prove Lemma
B.2
, Lemma
B.3
and Lemma
B.4
by induction.
When we prove Lemma
B.2
, we will assume that Lemma
B.3
and Lemma
B.4
hold at current iteration. The same situation still holds for proof of Lemma
B.3
and Lemma
B.4
. As we discussed earlier, this is not circular reasoning by the essence of induction. Rigorously, all the conclusions from these three lemmas could be composed into a big induction. However, for simplicity and consistency, we present them respectively. Besides, we denote
E_{t}
the event that
|\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle|\leq c_{4}D^{1/4}
and
\|\mathbf{x}_{j}\|_{2}\leq\sigma_{x}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})
for some constant
c_{4}
solely depending on
\eta,\sigma_{x}
and all
j\in[D]
, and denote
E_{t}^{c}
the complement event. By Lemma
B.5
, the occurrence of
E_{t}
can imply that

\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(t)}\geq 1-D\exp(-C_{9}D)
and
\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\leq\exp(-C_{9}D)
for all
j^{\prime}\neq j^{*}
and
j\in[D]
. And the probability of
E_{t}
follows that
\mathbb{P}\big(E_{s}^{(t)}\big)\geq 1-\exp(-C_{8}\sqrt{D})
.

In the following, we present the proof for Lemma
B.2
.

Proof of Lemma
B.2
.

By Lemma
B.11
and Lemma
B.12
, we have
\mathbf{W}_{1,1}^{(2)}=\beta_{1}\mathbf{v}^{*}\mathbf{v}^{*\top}
with
|\beta_{1}|\leq c_{1}\sqrt{D}
, and
\mathbf{W}_{2,2}^{(2)}=\beta_{2}\Big(\sum_{j\neq j^{*}}\big(\mathbf{p}_{j^{*}}-\mathbf{p}_{j}\big)\Big)\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\bigg)
with
c_{2}\frac{1}{D^{2}}\leq\beta_{2}\leq c_{3}\frac{1}{D^{2}}
, aligning with the formulas of
\mathbf{W}_{1,1}^{(t)},\mathbf{W}_{2,2}^{(t)}
in Lemma
B.3
and Lemma
B.4
. We assume the conclusions of Lemma
B.3
and Lemma
B.4
still hold for any
t<T^{*}
, then by Lemma
B.5
, we have
P\big(E_{s}^{(t)}\big)\geq 1-\exp(-C_{8}\sqrt{D})
.
Based on this result, we define a proxy gradient
\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(t)}
which is calculated by assuming

\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(t)}=1
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(t)}=D\mathbb{E}\big[\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+Dy\langle\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\big].

Besides, since
\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}
is perpendicular to
\mathbf{v}^{*}
(which is
\mathbf{0}
at
t=2
), it is inner the linear subspace spanned by the
\{\bm{\xi}_{2},\cdots,\bm{\xi}_{d}\}
, and we denote its decomposition as
\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}=\sum_{i=2}^{d}a_{i}^{(t)}\bm{\xi}_{i}
. By the orthogonality among
\bm{\xi}_{2},\cdots,\bm{\xi}_{d}
, we have
\sum_{i=2}^{d}\big(a_{i}^{(t)}\big)^{2}=\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}^{2}
. We also denote
\mathcal{P}_{\bm{\xi}}
the projection matrix of the linear subspace spanned by the
\{\bm{\xi}_{2},\cdots,\bm{\xi}_{d}\}
. Then, we can decompose
\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(t)}
by a similar process in Lemma
B.10
as

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=D\mathbb{E}\Big[\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+Dy\langle\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\Big]

\displaystyle=D\mathbf{A}\mathbf{A}^{\top}\mathbb{E}\Big[\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+Dy\sum_{i=2}^{d}a_{i}^{(t)}\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\Big]

\displaystyle=D\mathbf{A}\mathbb{E}\Big[\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+Dy\sum_{i=2}^{d}a_{i}^{(t)}\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\big[\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle,\langle\bm{\xi}_{2},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle,\cdots,\langle\bm{\xi}_{d},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big]^{\top}\Big]

\displaystyle=D\mathbb{E}\Big[\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+D\sum_{i=2}^{d}a_{i}^{(t)}y\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\Big]\mathbf{v}^{*}

\displaystyle\ +\sum_{i=2}^{d}D\mathbb{E}\Big[\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+D\sum_{i=2}^{d}a_{i}^{(t)}y\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\Big]\bm{\xi}_{i}.

And we can upper bound the difference term as

\displaystyle\Big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(t)},\mathbf{W}^{(t)})-\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(t)}\Big\|_{2}=\bigg\|\mathbb{E}\Big[D\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+Dy\langle\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}-\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\ell^{\prime(t)}y\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\Big]\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\bigg\|\Big(D\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+Dy\langle\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}-\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\ell^{\prime(t)}y\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\Big)\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\bigg\|_{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\bigg\|\Big(D\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+Dy\langle\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}-\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\ell^{\prime(t)}y\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\Big)\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\bigg\|_{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\underbrace{D\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big|\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+Dy\langle\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)-\ell^{\prime(t)}\Big|\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\|_{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\bigg]}_{I_{1}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\bigg(D-\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(t)}\bigg)\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\big\|_{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\Bigg]}_{I_{2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j^{*}}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\big\|_{2}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\Big]}_{I_{3}}+\underbrace{D\mathbb{E}\Big[\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\big\|_{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\Big]}_{I_{4}}+\underbrace{D\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\big\|_{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\Big]}_{I_{5}},

where the inequalities hold by triangle inequality and the fact that
|\ell^{\prime}|\leq 1
and
|\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}|\leq 1
. Next, we demonstrate our analysis on two cases:
t=2
and
t\geq 3
.

When
t=2
,
\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(2)}=\alpha^{(2)}\mathbf{v}^{*}
implies that
\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(2)}=\mathbf{0}
. Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(2)}=D\mathbb{E}\Big[\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(2)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\Big]\mathbf{v}^{*}.

And we provide the upper bounds for each term of
\Big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(2)},\mathbf{W}^{(2)})-\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(2)}\Big\|_{2}
respectively.
Specially, for
I_{1}
, we can derive that

\displaystyle I_{1}\leq

\displaystyle D\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big|\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+Dy\langle\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)-\ell^{\prime(2)}\Big|\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\|_{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{D}{4}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\bigg|D\alpha^{(2)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle-\alpha^{(2)}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j=1}^{D}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\bigg|\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\big\|_{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{D}{4}\big|\alpha^{(2)}\big|\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\bigg(D-\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(t)}\bigg)\big|\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big|\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\big\|_{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\Bigg]+\frac{D}{4}\big|\alpha^{(2)}\big|\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j^{*}}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big|\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\big\|_{2}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{c_{3}\sigma_{x}D^{\frac{13}{4}}\big|\alpha^{(2)}\big|(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})}{2e^{C_{9}D}}\leq\frac{1}{5\eta e^{C_{4}\sqrt{D}}}.

The first inequality is because
\ell^{\prime}
is Lipschitz continuous with
\frac{1}{4}
. The penultimate inequality holds since
|\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle|\leq c_{3}D^{1/4}
,
\|\mathbf{x}_{j}\|_{2}\leq\sigma_{x}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})
,

\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(t)}\geq 1-D\exp(-C_{9}D)
and
\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\leq\exp(-C_{9}D)
when
\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}=1
. And the last inequality holds since
\alpha^{(2)}=-\Theta(\sqrt{D})
. Similarly, we also have

\displaystyle I_{2}\leq\frac{\sigma_{x}D(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})}{e^{C_{9}D}}\leq\frac{1}{5\eta e^{C_{4}\sqrt{D}}};\quad\text{and}\quad I_{3}\leq\frac{\sigma_{x}D^{2}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})}{e^{cD}}\leq\frac{1}{5\eta e^{C_{4}\sqrt{D}}}.

For
I_{4}
and
I_{5}
, by applying Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle I_{4}\leq D\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\|_{2}^{2}\big]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}^{2}\big]}\leq\frac{D\sqrt{d}}{e^{\frac{C_{8}}{2}\sqrt{D}}}\leq\frac{1}{5\eta e^{C_{4}\sqrt{D}}};

\displaystyle I_{5}\leq D\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\big[\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\|_{2}^{2}\big]}\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}^{2}\big]}\leq\frac{D^{2}\sqrt{d}}{e^{\frac{C_{8}}{2}\sqrt{D}}}\leq\frac{1}{5\eta e^{C_{4}\sqrt{D}}}.

Combining all preceding results, we have
\big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(2)},\mathbf{W}^{(2)})-\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(2)}\big\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{e^{C_{4}\sqrt{D}}}
for some constant
C_{4}
only depending on
\sigma_{x}
and
\eta
. Furthermore, we can derive that

\displaystyle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(3)}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(2)}-\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(2)},\mathbf{W}^{(2)})=\alpha^{(2)}\mathbf{v}^{*}-\eta\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(2)}-\eta\Big(\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(2)},\mathbf{W}^{(2)})-\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(2)}\Big)

\displaystyle=\bigg(\alpha^{(2)}+D\eta\mathbb{E}\Big[-\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(2)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\Big]+\Big\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\eta\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(2)}-\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(2)},\mathbf{W}^{(2)})\Big\rangle\bigg)\mathbf{v}^{*}

\displaystyle\quad+\eta\mathcal{P}_{\bm{\xi}}\Big(\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(2)}-\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(2)},\mathbf{W}^{(2)})\Big)

\displaystyle=\alpha^{(3)}\mathbf{v}^{*}+\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(3)}.

For
\alpha^{(3)}
, we can utilize Lemma
D.10
to derive that

\displaystyle\alpha^{(3)}\geq\frac{\eta\sigma_{x}}{4}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}D+\alpha^{(2)}-\eta\big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(2)},\mathbf{W}^{(2)})-\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(2)}\big\|_{2}\geq\frac{\eta\sigma_{x}}{5}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}D;

\displaystyle\alpha^{(3)}\leq\eta\sigma_{x}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}D+\alpha^{(2)}+\eta\big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(2)},\mathbf{W}^{(2)})-\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(2)}\big\|_{2}\leq\eta\sigma_{x}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}D.

And we also have
\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(3)}\big\|_{2}\leq\eta\big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(2)},\mathbf{W}^{(2)})-\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(2)}\big\|_{2}\leq e^{-C_{4}\sqrt{D}}
, which completes the proof at
t=3
.

Since we have derived that (
B.5
) and (
B.4
) hold at
t=3
. We will use induction to prove the case when
3<t\leq T^{*}
. Instead of directly proving (
B.4
), we prove the following inequality

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\leq\frac{\eta t}{e^{c\sqrt{D}}},

(B.12)

where
c
is some positive constant solely depending on
\sigma_{x}
and
\eta
. Then we assume (
B.5
) and (
B.12
) hold at any
3<t\leq T^{*}-1
. Similarly, we can bound
I_{1}
in
\Big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(t)},\mathbf{W}^{(t)})-\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(t)}\Big\|_{2}
as

\displaystyle I_{1}\leq

\displaystyle D\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Big|\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+Dy\langle\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)-\ell^{\prime(t)}\Big|\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\|_{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{D}{4}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\bigg|D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+Dy\langle\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle-\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j=1}^{D}y\langle\alpha^{(t)}\mathbf{v}^{*}+\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\bigg|\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\big\|_{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{D\alpha^{(t)}}{4}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\bigg(D-\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(t)}\bigg)\big|\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big|\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\big\|_{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\Bigg]+\frac{D\alpha^{(t)}}{4}\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j^{*}}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big|\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\big\|_{2}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\Big]

\displaystyle+\frac{D}{4}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\bigg(D-\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(t)}\bigg)\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\big\|_{2}^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\Bigg]+\frac{D}{4}\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j^{*}}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\big\|_{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{c_{3}\sigma_{x}D^{\frac{13}{4}}\alpha^{(t)}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})+4\sigma_{x}^{2}D^{3}\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\max\{d,D\}}{2e^{C_{9}D}}\leq\frac{1}{5e^{c\sqrt{D}}}

The first inequality is because
\ell^{\prime}
is Lipschitz continuous with
\frac{1}{4}
. The penultimate inequality holds since
|\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle|\leq c_{3}D^{1/4}
,
\|\mathbf{x}_{j}\|_{2}\leq\sigma_{x}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})
,

\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(t)}\geq 1-D\exp(-C_{9}D)
and
\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\leq\exp(-C_{9}D)
when
\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}=1
. And the last inequality holds since
\alpha^{(t)}\leq O((T^{*})^{1/3}+D)\ll\exp(C_{9}D)
by (
B.5
) and our definition of
T^{*}
in Theorem
3.2
. For other terms in
\Big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(t)},\mathbf{W}^{(t)})-\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(t)}\Big\|_{2}
, we can obtain the same upper bound with
t=2
. Therefore, by combining these results, we obtain that
\big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(t)},\mathbf{W}^{(t)})-\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{e^{c\sqrt{D}}}
for some constant
c
only depending on
\sigma_{x}
and
\eta
. Now, we are ready to derive the gradient descent update for
\mathbf{v}^{(t+1)}
as

\displaystyle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t+1)}=

\displaystyle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)}-\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(t)},\mathbf{W}^{(t)})=\alpha^{(t)}\mathbf{v}^{*}+\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}-\eta\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(t)}-\eta\Big(\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(t)},\mathbf{W}^{(t)})-\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(t)}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg(\alpha^{(t)}+D\eta\mathbb{E}\Big[-\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+Dy\langle\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\Big]+\Big\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\eta\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(t)}-\eta\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}\Big\rangle\bigg)\mathbf{v}^{*}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=2}^{d}\bigg(a_{i}^{(t)}+\eta D\mathbb{E}\Big[-\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+D\sum_{i=2}^{d}a_{i}^{(t)}y\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\Big]\bigg)\bm{\xi}_{i}+\eta\mathcal{P}_{\bm{\xi}}\Big(\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(t)}-\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}\Big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha^{(t+1)}\mathbf{v}^{*}+\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t+1)}.

For
\alpha^{(t+1)}
, we can utilize Lemma
D.10
to derive the following iterative formulas

\displaystyle\alpha^{(t+1)}

\displaystyle\geq\alpha^{(t)}+\eta D\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\bigg(\frac{1}{\sigma_{x}D^{2}\big(\alpha^{(t)}\big)^{2}}-\frac{1}{\sigma_{x}^{3}D^{4}\big(\alpha^{(t)}\big)^{4}}\bigg)-\eta\big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(t)},\mathbf{W}^{(t)})-\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\alpha^{(t)}+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{\eta}{2\sigma_{x}D}\frac{1}{\big(\alpha^{(t)}\big)^{2}},

and

\displaystyle\alpha^{(t+1)}

\displaystyle\leq\alpha^{(t)}+\eta D\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{1}{\sigma_{x}D^{2}\big(\alpha^{(t)}\big)^{2}}e^{\frac{\sigma_{x}^{2}D^{2}\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{2}}+\eta\big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(t)},\mathbf{W}^{(t)})-\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\alpha^{(t)}+\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{2\eta}{\sigma_{x}D}\frac{1}{\big(\alpha^{(t)}\big)^{2}}

hold for all
t\geq 3
. Then combined with the fact about initialization that
\frac{\eta\sigma_{x}}{5}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}D\leq\alpha^{(3)}\leq\eta\sigma_{x}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}D
, Lemma
D.19
and comparison theorem, we finally get that

\displaystyle\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{3\eta}{2\sigma_{x}D}(t-2)+\frac{2\eta^{3}\sigma_{x}^{3}}{125\pi}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}D^{3}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{3}}\leq\alpha^{(t+1)}\leq\sqrt{\frac{\pi}{2}}\frac{2}{\eta\sigma_{x}^{3}D^{3}}+\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{6\eta}{\sigma_{x}D}(t-2)+\frac{2\eta^{3}\sigma_{x}^{3}}{\pi}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}D^{3}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{3}},

which completes the proof for (
B.5
). Next, we demonstrate the upper bound for
\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t+1)}\big\|_{2}
. In order to provide this result, we first analyze the coefficient of each
\bm{\xi}_{i}
, and W.L.O.G, we assume
a_{i}^{(t)}\geq 0
. Then by Lemma
D.11
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[-\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+Dy\langle\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\Big]\leq 0,

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Big[-\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+Dy\langle\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\Big]

\displaystyle\geq-\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}\frac{\sigma_{x}^{2}a_{i}^{(t)}}{\alpha^{(t)}}e^{\frac{\sigma_{x}^{2}D^{2}\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}^{2}}{2}}\geq-\frac{6\sigma_{x}a_{i}^{(t)}}{\eta D},

where the last inequality holds by
\alpha^{(t)}\geq\frac{\eta\sigma_{x}}{5}\sqrt{\frac{2}{\pi}}D
. Therefore the coefficient of
\bm{\xi}_{i}
follows that

\displaystyle\bigg|a_{i}^{(t)}+\eta D\mathbb{E}\Big[-\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+D\sum_{i=2}^{d}a_{i}^{(t)}y\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\Big]\bigg|\leq\big|a_{i}^{(t)}\big|,

by
\sigma_{x}\leq\frac{1}{3}
from conditions of Theorem
3.2

Based on these results, we can finally provide the upper-bound for
\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t+1)}\big\|_{2}
as

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t+1)}\big\|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\Bigg\|\sum_{i=2}^{d}\bigg(a_{i}^{(t)}+\eta D\mathbb{E}\Big[-\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+D\sum_{i=2}^{d}a_{i}^{(t)}y\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\Big]\bigg)\bm{\xi}_{i}\Bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\eta\Big\|\mathcal{G}_{v}^{(t)}-\nabla_{\mathbf{v}_{1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(t)},\mathbf{W}^{(t)})\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{i=2}^{d}\bigg(a_{i}^{(t)}+\eta D\mathbb{E}\Big[-\ell^{\prime}\big(D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+D\sum_{i=2}^{d}a_{i}^{(t)}y\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\big)y\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\Big]\bigg)^{2}}+\frac{\eta}{e^{c\sqrt{D}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{i=2}^{d}\big(a_{i}^{(t)}\big)^{2}}+\frac{\eta}{e^{c\sqrt{D}}}=\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}+\frac{\eta}{e^{c\sqrt{D}}}\leq\frac{(t+1)\eta}{e^{c\sqrt{D}}},

which completes the proof for (
B.12
). Further by our definition of
T^{*}
in Theorem
3.2
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\leq\frac{T^{*}\eta}{e^{c\sqrt{D}}}\leq e^{-C_{4}\sqrt{D}}.

This completes the proof for (
B.4
).

∎

Next, we present the proof for Lemma
B.3
.

Proof of Lemma
B.3
.

By calculations in Lemma
B.11
, we have obtained that
\mathbf{W}_{1,1}^{(2)}
follows (
B.6
) with
\mathbf{W}_{1,1,\text{error}}^{(2)}=0
. Instead of directly proving (
B.7
), we prove the following inequality,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{W}_{1,1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\|_{2}\leq\frac{\eta t}{e^{c\sqrt{D}}}.

(B.13)

for some constant
c
solely depending on
\eta
and
\sigma_{x}
.
Therefore by induction, we assume it holds for
t
, and prove it still holds for
t+1
. Actually, it suffices to show that
\big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{W}_{1,1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(t)},\mathbf{W}^{(t)})\big\|_{2}\leq e^{-c\sqrt{D}}
. By (
B.2
), we have

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{W}_{1,1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(t)},\mathbf{W}^{(t)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\not=j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle(\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}-\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}})\mathbf{x}_{j}^{\top}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\mathbf{x}_{j}^{\top}\bigg]}_{I_{1}}-\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\not=j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\mathbf{x}_{j}^{\top}\bigg]}_{I_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\mathbf{x}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\bigg]}_{I_{1,1}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\mathbf{x}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\bigg]}_{I_{1,2}}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\not=j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\mathbf{x}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\bigg]}_{I_{2,1}}-\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\not=j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\mathbf{x}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\bigg]}_{I_{2,2}}

For
I_{1,1}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\|I_{1,1}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)\Big(\alpha^{(t)}\big|\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big|+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\big\|_{2}\Big)\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j}\big\|_{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8D^{2}\max\{d,D\}\big(c_{3}\alpha^{(t)}D^{1/4}+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})\big)}{e^{c_{9}D}}\leq\frac{1}{4e^{c\sqrt{D}}}.

The first inequality is by triangle inequality and
|y|,|\ell^{\prime}|\leq 1
. The second inequality holds because
|\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle|\leq c_{3}D^{1/4}
,
\|\mathbf{x}_{j}\|_{2}\leq\sigma_{x}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})
,

\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(t)}\geq 1-D\exp(-cD)
and
\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\leq\exp(-cD)
when
\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}=1
. The second inequality holds since
\alpha^{(t)}\leq O((T^{*})^{1/3}+D)\ll\exp(C_{9}D)
and
\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\leq e^{-C_{4}\sqrt{D}}
by Lemma
B.2
and definition of
T^{*}
in Theorem
3.2
. Similarly, for
I_{2,1}
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|I_{2,1}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\neq j^{\prime}}^{D}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}\Big(\alpha^{(t)}\big|\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big|+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\big\|_{2}\Big)\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j}\big\|_{2}\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{9D^{2}\max\{d,D\}\big(c_{3}\alpha^{(t)}D^{1/4}+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})\big)}{e^{cD}}\leq\frac{1}{4e^{c\sqrt{D}}}.

In the following, we also denote
\mathbf{v}^{*}
by
\bm{\xi}_{1}
in summation calculation for simplicity of expression. Then by applying Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we can obtain an upper bound as

\displaystyle\big\|I_{1,2}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg\|\mathbf{A}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\begin{bmatrix}\langle\bm{\xi}_{1},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\\
\langle\bm{\xi}_{2},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\\
\vdots\\
\langle\bm{\xi}_{d},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\end{bmatrix}\big[\langle\bm{\xi}_{1},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle,\langle\bm{\xi}_{2},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle,\cdots,\langle\bm{\xi}_{d},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\bigg]\mathbf{A}^{\top}\Bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\bigg]\bm{\xi}_{i}\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}}\Bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\bigg]^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\bigg)^{2}\Bigg]}\sqrt{\mathbb{P}(E_{t}^{c})}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\langle\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\bigg)^{2}\bigg(\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\bigg)\Bigg]}\sqrt{\mathbb{P}(E_{t}^{c})}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\big|\langle\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big|\bigg)^{2}\bigg(\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\bigg)\Bigg]}\sqrt{\mathbb{P}(E_{t}^{c})}

\displaystyle\leq\big(\alpha^{(t)}+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\big)\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{D}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\langle\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}},\mathbf{x}_{j_{1}}\rangle\big|\big|\langle\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}},\mathbf{x}_{j_{2}}\rangle\big|\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)}/\|\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)}\|_{2},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\Big]}\sqrt{\mathbb{P}(E_{t}^{c})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{15}\sigma_{x}^{3}dD^{\frac{3}{2}}\big(\alpha^{(t)}+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\big)}{e^{C_{8}\sqrt{D}}}\leq\frac{1}{4e^{c\sqrt{D}}}.

The first and second inequality is derived by Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, and the facts that
\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\big(\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)^{2}\leq 1
and
\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)^{2},y^{2},\ell^{\prime 2}\leq 1
. The last inequality holds since
\alpha^{(t)}\leq O((T^{*})^{1/3}+D)\ll\exp(C_{8}\sqrt{D})
and
\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\leq e^{-C_{4}\sqrt{D}}
by Lemma
B.2
and definition of
T^{*}
in Theorem
3.2
. Similarly for
I_{2,2}
, we also have

\displaystyle\big\|I_{2,2}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg\|\mathbf{A}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j\neq j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\begin{bmatrix}\langle\bm{\xi}_{1},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle\\
\langle\bm{\xi}_{2},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle\\
\vdots\\
\langle\bm{\xi}_{d},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle\end{bmatrix}\big[\langle\bm{\xi}_{1},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle,\langle\bm{\xi}_{2},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle,\cdots,\langle\bm{\xi}_{d},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big]\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\bigg]\mathbf{A}^{\top}\Bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg\|\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j\neq j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\bigg]\bm{\xi}_{i}\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}}\Bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j\neq j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\bigg]^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j\neq j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle\langle\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\bigg)^{2}\Bigg]}\sqrt{\mathbb{P}(E_{t}^{c})}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\langle\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\bigg)^{2}\bigg(\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}=1}^{D}\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle^{2}\bigg)\Bigg]}\sqrt{\mathbb{P}(E_{t}^{c})}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\big|\langle\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle\big|\bigg)^{2}\bigg(\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\bigg)\bigg(\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}=1}^{D}\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle^{2}\bigg)\Bigg]}\sqrt{\mathbb{P}(E_{t}^{c})}

\displaystyle=\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{d}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{D}\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big|\langle\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}},\mathbf{x}_{j_{1}}\rangle\big|\big|\langle\bm{\xi}_{i^{\prime}},\mathbf{x}_{j_{2}}\rangle\big|\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)}/\|\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)}\|_{2},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\langle\bm{\xi}_{i},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\rangle^{2}\Big]}\sqrt{\mathbb{P}(E_{t}^{c})}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sqrt{15}\sigma_{x}^{3}dD^{2}\big(\alpha^{(t)}+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\big)}{e^{C_{8}\sqrt{D}}}\leq\frac{1}{4e^{c\sqrt{D}}}.

Combining all the preceding results, we have

\displaystyle\Big\|\mathbf{W}_{1,1,\text{error}}^{(t+1)}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\mathbf{W}_{1,1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\Big\|_{2}+\eta\big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{W}_{1,1}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(t)},\mathbf{W}^{(t)})\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\mathbf{W}_{1,1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\Big\|_{2}+\eta\big\|I_{1,1}\big\|_{2}+\eta\big\|I_{1,2}\big\|_{2}+\eta\big\|I_{2,1}\big\|_{2}+\eta\big\|I_{2,2}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta t}{e^{c\sqrt{D}}}+\frac{4\eta}{4e^{c\sqrt{D}}}=\frac{\eta(t+1)}{e^{c\sqrt{D}}},

which completes the proof for (
B.13
). Further by our definition of
T^{*}
in Theorem
3.2
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\Big\|\mathbf{W}_{1,1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\Big\|_{2}\leq\frac{T^{*}\eta}{e^{c\sqrt{D}}}\leq e^{-C_{7}\sqrt{D}}.

This completes the proof for (
B.7
).
∎

Next, we present the proof for Lemma
B.4
.

Proof of Lemma
B.4
.

By calculations in Lemma
B.12
, we have obtained that
\mathbf{W}_{2,2}^{(2)}
follows (
B.8
) with
\mathbf{W}_{2,2,\text{error}}^{(2)}=0
.
Instead of directly proving (
B.9
), we prove the following inequality,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{W}_{2,2,\text{error}}^{(t)}\|_{2}\leq\frac{\eta t}{e^{c\sqrt{D}}}.

(B.14)

for some constant
c
solely depending on
\eta
and
\sigma_{x}
. Therefore by induction, we assume it holds for
t
, and prove it still holds for
t+1
. Actually, it suffices to show that
\big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{W}_{2,2}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(t)},\mathbf{W}^{(t)})\big\|_{2}\leq e^{-c\sqrt{D}}
. By (
B.2
), we have

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{W}_{2,2}}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{v}^{(t)},\mathbf{W}^{(t)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\not=j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle(\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}-\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime\prime}})\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\bigg]}_{I_{1}}-\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\ell^{\prime(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\not=j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\bigg]}_{I_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\Big[\ell^{\prime(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\Big]\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}}_{I_{1,1}}+\underbrace{\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\Big[\ell^{\prime(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\Big]\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}}_{I_{1,2}}

\displaystyle-\underbrace{\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\Big[\ell^{\prime(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\Big]\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}}_{I_{2,1}}-\underbrace{\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\not=j^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\ell^{\prime(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\Big]\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}}_{I_{2,2}}

For
I_{1,1}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\|I_{1,1}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)\Big(\alpha^{(t)}\big|\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\big|+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\big\|_{2}\Big)\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\Big]\big\|\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{p}_{j}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D^{2}(D+1)\big(c_{3}\alpha^{(t)}D^{1/4}+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})\big)}{e^{C_{9}D}}\leq\frac{1}{4e^{c\sqrt{D}}}.

The first inequality is by triangle inequality and
|y|,|\ell^{\prime}|\leq 1
. The second inequality holds because
|\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}\rangle|\leq c_{3}D^{1/4}
,
\|\mathbf{x}_{j}\|_{2}\leq\sigma_{x}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})
,

\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(t)}\geq 1-D\exp(-C_{9}D)
and
\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\leq\exp(-C_{9}D)
when
\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}=1
, and
\|\mathbf{p}_{j}\|_{2}=\sqrt{(D+1)/2}
by Lemma
D.18
. The second inequality holds since
\alpha^{(t)}\leq O((T^{*})^{1/3}+D)\ll\exp(C_{9}D)
and
\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\leq e^{-C_{4}\sqrt{D}}
by Lemma
B.2
and definition of
T^{*}
in Theorem
3.2
. Similarly, for
I_{2,1}
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|I_{2,1}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\Big[\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}\Big(\alpha^{(t)}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\big\|_{2}\Big)\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}\}}\Big]\big\|\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{p}_{j}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2D^{2}(D+1)\big(c_{3}\alpha^{(t)}D^{1/4}+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})\big)}{e^{C_{9}D}}\leq\frac{1}{4e^{c\sqrt{D}}}.

By applying Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we can obtain an upper bound for
I_{1,2}
as

\displaystyle\big\|I_{1,2}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg\|\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\Big[\ell^{\prime(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\Big]\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\Bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\Big[\ell^{\prime(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\Big]^{2}\big\|\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big\|\mathbf{p}_{j}\big\|_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(D+1)\sqrt{\mathbb{P}(E_{t}^{c})}}{2}\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\Big[\big(\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)^{2}\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\Big]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(D+1)\big(\alpha^{(t)}+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\big)\sqrt{\mathbb{P}(E_{t}^{c})}}{2}\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sqrt{\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)}/\|\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)}\|_{2},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{4}\big]}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3^{\frac{1}{4}}\sigma_{x}(D+1)D^{\frac{3}{4}}\big(\alpha^{(t)}+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\big)}{2e^{C_{8}\sqrt{D}}}\leq\frac{1}{4e^{c\sqrt{D}}}.

The first inequality is derived by Cauchy–Schwarz inequality,
\big\|\mathbf{p}_{j}\big\|_{2}=\sqrt{(D+1)/2}
by Lemma
D.18
, The second inequality is derived by Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, and
\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\big(\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)^{4}\leq 1
. The last inequality holds since
\alpha^{(t)}\leq O((T^{*})^{1/3}+D)\ll\exp(C_{8}\sqrt{D})
and
\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\leq e^{-C_{4}\sqrt{D}}
by Lemma
B.2
and definition of
T^{*}
in Theorem
3.2
. Similarly for
I_{2,2}
, we also have

\displaystyle\big\|I_{2,2}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Bigg\|\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\neq j^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\ell^{\prime(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\Big]\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime\prime}}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\Bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\neq j^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}\Big[\ell^{\prime(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{1}_{\{E_{t}^{c}\}}\Big]^{2}\big\|\mathbf{p}_{j^{\prime}}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big\|\mathbf{p}_{j}\big\|_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(D+1)\sqrt{\mathbb{P}(E_{t}^{c})}}{2}\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\big(\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)^{2}\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\neq j^{\prime}}\big(\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)^{2}\bigg]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(D+1)\big(\alpha^{(t)}+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\big)\sqrt{\mathbb{P}(E_{t}^{c})}}{2}\sqrt{\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sqrt{\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbb{E}\big[\langle\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)}/\|\mathbf{v}_{1}^{(t)}\|_{2},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{4}\big]}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3^{\frac{1}{4}}\sigma_{x}(D+1)D^{\frac{3}{4}}\big(\alpha^{(t)}+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\big)}{2e^{C_{8}\sqrt{D}}}\leq\frac{1}{4e^{c\sqrt{D}}}.

Combining all the preceding results, we have

\displaystyle\Big\|\mathbf{W}_{2,2,\text{error}}^{(t+1)}\Big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\mathbf{W}_{2,2,\text{error}}^{(t)}\Big\|_{2}+\eta\big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{W}_{2,2}^{(t)}}\mathcal{L}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\Big\|\mathbf{W}_{2,2,\text{error}}^{(t)}\Big\|_{2}+\eta\big\|I_{1,1}\big\|_{2}+\eta\big\|I_{1,2}\big\|_{2}+\eta\big\|I_{2,1}\big\|_{2}+\eta\big\|I_{2,2}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\eta t}{e^{c\sqrt{D}}}+\frac{4\eta}{4e^{c\sqrt{D}}}=\frac{\eta(t+1)}{e^{c\sqrt{D}}},

which completes the proof for (
B.14
). Further by our definition of
T^{*}
in Theorem
3.2
, we can obtain

\displaystyle\Big\|\mathbf{W}_{2,2,\text{error}}^{(t)}\Big\|_{2}\leq\frac{T^{*}\eta}{e^{c\sqrt{D}}}\leq e^{-C_{7}\sqrt{D}}.

This completes the proof for (
B.9
).
∎

Finally, we provide the proof for Lemma
5.3

Proof of Lemma
5.3
.

By Lemma
B.2
and Proposition
B.1
, we can re-write
yf(\mathbf{Z},\mathbf{W}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{(t)})
as

\displaystyle yf(\mathbf{Z},\mathbf{W}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{(t)})=\alpha^{(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}+\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}y\big\langle\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\big\rangle\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}.

By the fact that
\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\big(\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)^{2}\leq 1
and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we can get a worst-case lower bound for
yf(\mathbf{Z},\mathbf{W}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{(t)})
as

\displaystyle yf(\mathbf{Z},\mathbf{W}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{(t)})\geq

\displaystyle-\alpha^{(t)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\Bigg(\bigg(\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg(\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\big(\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)^{2}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle-\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\Bigg(\bigg(\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\Big\langle\frac{\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}}{\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\|_{2}},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\Big\rangle^{2}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\bigg(\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\big(\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\big)^{2}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle-D\alpha^{(t)}\bigg(\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}\bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}-D\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\Bigg(\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\bigg\langle\frac{\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}}{\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\bigg\rangle^{2}\Bigg)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Since
\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\rangle=0
, we can derive that
\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2}
and
\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\Big\langle\frac{\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}}{\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\|_{2}},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\Big\rangle^{2}
are i.i.d. random variables and
\frac{1}{\sigma_{x}^{2}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\rangle^{2},\frac{1}{\sigma_{x}^{2}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\Big\langle\frac{\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}}{\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\|_{2}},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\Big\rangle^{2}\sim\chi_{D}^{2}
by the properties of Gaussian distribution.
Besides, when
E_{t}
holds, we also have

\displaystyle yf(\mathbf{Z},\mathbf{W}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{(t)})

\displaystyle\geq\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(t)}-\alpha^{(t)}\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j^{*}}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\big\|_{2}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}-\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\big\|_{2}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\geq D\alpha^{(t)}\bigg(1-\frac{D}{e^{C_{9}D}}\bigg)y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle-\frac{\sigma_{x}\alpha^{(t)}D^{2}\big(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D}\big)}{e^{C_{9}D}}-\frac{\sigma_{x}D\big(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D}\big)}{e^{C_{4}\sqrt{D}}}

\displaystyle\geq D\alpha^{(t)}\bigg(1-\frac{D}{e^{cD}}\bigg)y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle-1.

The second inequality holds because
E_{t}
implies that
\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(t)}\geq 1-De^{-C_{9}D}
,
\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}\leq e^{-C_{9}D}
, and
\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\|_{2}\leq\sigma_{x}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})
by Lemma
B.5
and Lemma
D.16
. Besides, Lemma
5.2
guarantees that
\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\leq\frac{1}{e^{C_{4}\sqrt{D}}}
. The last inequality holds because
\alpha^{(t)}\leq O\big((T^{*})^{1/3}+D\big)
by Lemma
B.2
, which is much smaller than
e^{C_{9}D}
by our definition of
T^{*}
in Theorem
3.2
. Therefore, the last two terms are much smaller than
1
. Similarly, we can also obtain that

\displaystyle yf(\mathbf{Z},\mathbf{W}^{(t)},\mathbf{v}^{(t)})

\displaystyle\leq\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(t)}+\alpha^{(t)}\sum_{j^{\prime}\neq j^{*}}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\big\|_{2}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}+\big\|\mathbf{v}_{1,\text{error}}^{(t)}\big\|_{2}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\big\|\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}\big\|_{2}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(t)}

\displaystyle\leq D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+\frac{\sigma_{x}\alpha^{(t)}D^{2}\big(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D}\big)}{e^{C_{9}D}}+\frac{\sigma_{x}D\big(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D}\big)}{e^{C_{4}\sqrt{D}}}

\displaystyle\leq D\alpha^{(t)}y\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}\rangle+1.

This completes the proof.
∎

Appendix C
Proof of Theorem
4.2

This section provides a complete proof for Theorem
4.2
. Before we demonstrate the proof for Theorem
4.2
, we first introduce and prove several lemmas which will be utilized for further proof. The following two lemmas are very similar to Lemma
B.3
, Lemma
B.4
and Lemma
B.5
.

Lemma C.1
.

Under the same conditions of Theorem
4.2
, with probability at least
1-ne^{-C_{3}\sqrt{D}}
over the randomness of
\big(\mathbf{X}^{(i)}y^{(i)}\big)_{i=1}^{n}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(i)}=\begin{bmatrix}\beta_{1}\mathbf{v}^{*}\mathbf{v}^{*\top}&\mathbf{0}\\
\mathbf{0}&\beta_{2}\Big(\sum_{j\neq j^{*}}\big(\mathbf{p}_{j^{*}}-\mathbf{p}_{j}\big)\Big)\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\mathbf{p}_{j}^{\top}\bigg)\end{bmatrix}+\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathrm{error}}^{(i)},

(C.1)

where
|\beta_{1}|\leq c_{1}\sqrt{D}
,
\beta_{2}\geq\frac{c_{2}}{D^{2}}
for some constant
c_{1},c_{2}
solely depending on
\eta,\sigma_{x}
, and the error term satisfies that

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathrm{error}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}\leq\exp\big(-C_{10}\sqrt{D}\big).

(C.2)

The coefficients
C_{3},C_{10}
are both positive constants solely depending on
\sigma_{x},\widetilde{\sigma}_{x}
and
\eta
.

Lemma C.2
.

Under the same conditions of Theorem
4.2
, then with probability at least
1-ne^{-C_{3}\sqrt{D}}
over the randomness of
\big(\mathbf{X}^{(i)}y^{(i)}\big)_{i=1}^{n}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(i)}\geq 1-D\exp(-C_{11}D);

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(i)}\leq\exp(-C_{11}D)

for all
i\in[n]
,
j\in[D]
and
j^{\prime}\neq j^{*}
. The coefficients
C_{3},C_{11}
are both positive constants solely depending on
\sigma_{x},\widetilde{\sigma}_{x}
and
\eta
.

Proof of Lemma
C.2
.

By Lemma
C.1
, there exists constants
c_{1},c_{2}
solely depending on
\eta,\sigma_{x}
such that
|\beta_{1}|\leq c_{1}\sqrt{D}
and
\beta_{2}\geq c_{2}\frac{1}{D^{2}}
. Then further combining with Lemma
D.17
and Lemma
D.18
, with probability at least
1-ne^{-C_{1}\sqrt{D}}
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\big(\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big)^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(i)}\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}

\displaystyle=\beta_{1}\big\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big\rangle\big\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}^{(i)}\big\rangle-\frac{(D+1)^{2}}{4}\beta_{2}+\big(\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big)^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathrm{error}}^{(i)}\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}

\displaystyle\leq\Big|\beta_{1}\big\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big\rangle\big\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}^{(i)}\big\rangle\Big|+\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathrm{error}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq c_{1}\sqrt{D}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{c_{2}}{13c_{1}\widetilde{\sigma}_{x}^{2}}}\widetilde{\sigma}_{x}D^{\frac{1}{4}}\Bigg)^{2}+\frac{2\widetilde{\sigma}_{x}^{2}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})^{2}}{e^{C_{10}\sqrt{D}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{c_{2}}{12}D

holds for all
j^{\prime}\neq j^{*}
,
j\in[D]
and
i\in[D]
, and

\displaystyle\big(\mathbf{z}_{j^{*}}^{(i)}\big)^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(i)}\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}

\displaystyle=\beta_{1}\big\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}^{(i)}\big\rangle\big\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}^{(i)}\big\rangle+\frac{(D-1)(D+1)^{2}}{4}\beta_{2}+\big(\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big)^{\top}\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathrm{error}}^{(i)}\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{c_{2}D}{4}-\Big|\beta_{1}\big\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}^{(i)}\big\rangle\big\langle\mathbf{v}^{*},\mathbf{x}_{j}^{(i)}\big\rangle\Big|-\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathrm{error}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{c_{2}D}{4}-c_{1}\sqrt{D}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{c_{2}}{13c_{1}\sigma_{x}^{2}}}\sigma_{x}D^{\frac{1}{4}}\Bigg)^{2}-\frac{2\widetilde{\sigma}_{x}^{2}(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D})^{2}}{e^{C_{10}\sqrt{D}}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{c_{2}}{6}D

holds for all
j\in[D]
and
i\in[D]
. Therefore, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(i)}\geq\frac{e^{\frac{c_{2}}{6}D}}{e^{\frac{c_{2}}{6}D}+(D-1)e^{\frac{c_{2}}{12}D}}\geq 1-De^{-\frac{c_{2}}{12}D};

\displaystyle\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(i)}\leq\frac{e^{\frac{c_{2}}{12}D}}{e^{\frac{c_{2}}{6}D}+(D-1)e^{\frac{c_{2}}{12}D}}\leq e^{-\frac{c_{2}}{12}D}.

This completes the proof.
∎

Proof of Lemma
C.1
.

By Lemma
B.3
and Lemma
B.4
, we have obtained that
\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(1)}=\mathbf{W}^{(T^{*})}
follows (
C.1
) with
\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\text{error}}^{(1)}\big\|_{2}\leq\max\Big\{\big\|\mathbf{W}_{1,1,\text{error}}^{(T^{*})}\big\|_{2},\big\|\mathbf{W}_{2,2,\text{error}}^{(T^{*})}\big\|_{2}\Big\}\leq\exp\big(-C_{7}\sqrt{D}\big)
. Instead of directly proving (
C.2
), we prove the following inequality,

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\mathrm{error}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}\leq\exp\big(-C_{7}\sqrt{D}\big)+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}i}{\exp(cD)},

(C.3)

where
c
is a constant solely depending on
\sigma_{x},\widetilde{\sigma}_{x},\eta
.
Based on our previous discussion, we know it holds for
i=1
. Therefore by induction, we assume it holds for
i\leq n-1
, and prove it still holds for
i+1
. Actually, it suffices to show that
\big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{W}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{i}(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(i)})\big\|_{2}\leq e^{-cD}
. And we firstly provide an upper-bound for
\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}
as

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\widetilde{\eta}\sum_{k=1}^{i}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\ell^{\prime(i)}y^{(i)}\cdot\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}\mathbf{S}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(t)}\bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big(\sqrt{2}\widetilde{\sigma}_{x}+1\big)\widetilde{\eta}nD\big(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D}\big)\leq\frac{n}{\big(\sqrt{2}\widetilde{\sigma}_{x}+1\big)D\big(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D}\big)}.

The first inequality is because
\|\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}\|\leq\big(\sqrt{2}\widetilde{\sigma}_{x}+1\big)\big(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D}\big)
by Lemma
D.17
and Lemma
D.18
. The last inequality holds by the scale of
\widetilde{\eta}
from the conditions of Theorem
4.2
. Based on this upper-bound, we can further demonstrate the upper-bound of
\big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{W}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{i}(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(i)})\big\|_{2}

Then by (
B.2
), we have

\displaystyle\nabla_{\mathbf{W}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{i}(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(i)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ell^{\prime(i)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\not=j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(i)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(i)}y\big\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)},\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big\rangle\big(\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime}}^{(i)}-\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime\prime}}^{(i)}\big)\big(\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}\big)^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\ell^{\prime(i)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(i)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(i)}\big)y\big\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)},\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big\rangle\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big(\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}\big)^{\top}}_{I_{1}}-\underbrace{\ell^{\prime(i)}\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\not=j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(i)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(i)}y\big\langle\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)},\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big\rangle\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime\prime}}^{(i)}\big(\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}\big)^{\top}}_{I_{2}}.

For
I_{1}
, we have

\displaystyle\big\|I_{1}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(i)}\big(1-\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(i)}\big)\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}^{2}\big\|\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}\leq\frac{2\big(\sqrt{2}\widetilde{\sigma}_{x}+1\big)^{2}Dn\big(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D}\big)^{2}}{e^{C_{11}D}}\leq\frac{1}{2e^{cD}}.

The first inequality is by triangle inequality and
|y|,|\ell^{\prime}|\leq 1
. The second inequality holds because
\|\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}\|\leq\big(\sqrt{2}\widetilde{\sigma}_{x}+1\big)\big(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D}\big)
,

\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j}^{(i)}\geq 1-D\exp(-C_{11}D)
and
\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(i)}\leq\exp(-C_{11}D)
by Lemma
C.2
and upper-bound for
\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}
. The second inequality holds by the condition concerning
n
from Theorem
4.2
. Similarly, for
I_{2}
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\big\|I_{2}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime\prime}\neq j^{\prime}}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime},j}^{(i)}\mathbf{S}_{j^{\prime\prime},j}^{(i)}\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{z}_{j^{\prime\prime}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}\big\|\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}\leq\frac{2\big(\sqrt{2}\widetilde{\sigma}_{x}+1\big)^{2}Dn\big(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{D}\big)^{2}}{e^{C_{11}D}}\leq\frac{1}{2e^{cD}}.

Combining all the preceding results, we have

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\text{error}}^{(i+1)}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\text{error}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}+\widetilde{\eta}\big\|\nabla_{\mathbf{W}}\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{i}(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(i)})\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\text{error}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}+\widetilde{\eta}\big\|I_{1}\big\|_{2}+\widetilde{\eta}\big\|I_{2}\big\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\exp\big(-C_{7}\sqrt{D}\big)+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}i}{e^{cD}}+\frac{2\widetilde{\eta}}{2e^{cD}}=\exp\big(-C_{7}\sqrt{D}\big)+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}(i+1)}{e^{cD}},

which proves that (
C.3
). Then by conditions concerning
n
from Theorem
4.2
, we further have

\displaystyle\big\|\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}_{\text{error}}^{(i)}\big\|_{2}\leq\exp\big(-C_{7}\sqrt{D}\big)+\frac{\widetilde{\eta}i}{e^{cD}}\leq\exp(-C_{10}\sqrt{D}).

This completes the proof.
∎

For further proof in this section, we introduce the following several notations. We denote
\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{*}=\mathop{\mathrm{argmax}}_{\mathbf{v}:\|\mathbf{v}\|_{2}\leq 1}\min_{i\in[n]}y^{(i)}\cdot\big\langle\mathbf{v},\mathbf{x}_{j^{*}}^{(i)}\big\rangle
, and
\widehat{\mathbf{v}}=\big[(\frac{\log n}{\gamma D}\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{*})^{\top},\mathbf{0_{D}}^{\top}\big]^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{d+D}
. And we define
\widehat{\mathbf{W}}
is an idealized matrix such that
\mathbf{S}^{(i)}_{j^{*},j}=1
and
\mathbf{S}^{(i)}_{j^{\prime},j}=0
for all
j^{\prime}\neq j^{*}
and
j\in[D]
(Such a matrix exists by Lemma
D.18
). Furthermore, we define a proxy loss by
-\ell^{\prime}
as
\mathcal{R}_{i}(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}})=-\ell^{\prime}(y^{(i)}\cdot f(\mathbf{Z}^{(i)},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}},\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}))
and
\mathcal{R}(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}})=\mathbb{E}_{(\mathbf{X},y)\sim\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}}\big[-\ell^{\prime}(y\cdot f(\mathbf{Z},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}},\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}))\big]
. Based on these notations, we can finish the remaining proof. Firstly, we prove that
\widetilde{\mathcal{L}}_{i}(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}})
is nearly convex.

Lemma C.3
.

With probability at least
1-ne^{-C_{3}\sqrt{D}}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{v}},\widehat{\mathbf{W}})-\mathcal{L}_{i}(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(i)})\geq\big\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{v}}\mathcal{L}_{i}(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(i)}),\widehat{\mathbf{v}}-\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)}\big\rangle-\frac{1}{\gamma e^{C_{12}D}}

for all
i\in[n]
, where
C_{12}
is a constant solely depending on
\sigma_{x},\widetilde{\sigma}_{x},\eta

Proof of Lemma
C.3
.

By convexity of
\ell
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}_{i}(\widehat{\mathbf{v}},\widehat{\mathbf{W}})-\mathcal{L}_{i}(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(i)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ell\big(y^{(i)}f(\mathbf{Z}^{(i)},\widehat{\mathbf{W}},\widehat{\mathbf{v}})\big)-\ell\big(y^{(i)}f(\mathbf{Z}^{(i)},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(i)},\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)})\big)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\ell^{\prime}\big(y^{(i)}f(\mathbf{Z}^{(i)},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(i)},\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)})\big)y^{(i)}\big(f(\mathbf{Z}^{(i)},\widehat{\mathbf{W}},\widehat{\mathbf{v}})-f(\mathbf{Z}^{(i)},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(i)},\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)})\big)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ell^{\prime(i)}y^{(i)}\bigg(\sum_{j=1}^{D}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big\langle\widehat{\mathbf{v}}-\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)},\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}\big\rangle\bigg)+\ell^{\prime(i)}y^{(i)}\bigg(D-\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\bigg)\big\langle\widehat{\mathbf{v}},\mathbf{z}_{j^{*}}^{(i)}\big\rangle+\ell^{\prime(i)}y^{(i)}\sum_{j\neq j^{*}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big\langle\widehat{\mathbf{v}},\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}\big\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\big\langle\nabla_{\mathbf{v}}\mathcal{L}_{i}(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)},\widetilde{\mathbf{W}}^{(i)}),\widehat{\mathbf{v}}-\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}^{(i)}\big\rangle+\underbrace{\ell^{\prime(i)}y^{(i)}\bigg(D-\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}\mathbf{S}_{j^{*},j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\bigg)\big\langle\widehat{\mathbf{v}},\mathbf{z}_{j^{*}}^{(i)}\big\rangle+\ell^{\prime(i)}y^{(i)}\sum_{j\neq j^{*}}\sum_{j^{\prime}=1}^{D}\mathbf{S}_{j,j^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big\langle\widehat{\mathbf{v}},\mathbf{z}_{j}^{(i)}\big\rangle}_{I}

And we can bound
|I|
as

\displaystyle|I|
